### Better full gradient: top-tail + Lipschitz line search
Implement loop:
```
def top_tail_adam(parent, duration, rng, sigma_ratio=(0.05,0.2), lr0=0.02, ...):
    p=normalize(parent)
    z=log(p) ...
    m,v = zeros
    best=score(p)
    while time:
       b = conv(p)
       M = max(b)
       # maybe exact score
       # choose sigma = rng? dynamic
       z = (b-M)/sigma  # M shift
       w = softmax(z)  # length 2n-1
       g = 2 * corr(w, p)  # via FFT
       gz = g - p * dot(p,g)
       adam step
       p=softmax(z)
       evaluate score occasionally
```

```
def conv_full(p, L=None):
    n = p.size
    if L is None: L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0) # small negatives? Use np.abs? Convolution of nonnegative should nonneg, but due numerical maybe tiny negative; for gradient use nonneg? For b weights, max could be negative? set max 0.
    return c
```
Actually for objective max, if numerical negative small, max unaffected unless all negative (not possible). Set `np.maximum(c,0.0)`. But if there are actual negative due product? convolution of nonnegative can't be negative. Fine.

For score:
```
def fast_score(p):
   p=normalize(p); if None inf
   b=conv_full(p)
   M=b.max()
   return 2*p.size*M
```
Need if p all zeros? normalize handles.

Gradient for weights:
```
def gradient_weights(p, w, L=None):
    n=p.size
    if L is None: L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Wref = w[::-1]
    # pad to L by rfft automatically with zeros
    G = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
    g = 2.0 * G[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    if not finite: g=0
    return g
```
For top-tail target, w length 2n-1 nonnegative sum maybe 1.

But computing `Wref = w[::-1]` creates view; rfft okay. We also need P for b. We can combine in loop to avoid recomputing P for b and gradient. Use a function `score_and_grad_weights(p, L=None, w=None, current_b=None)`? To reduce.

In optimization loop:
```
Lscore = next_pow2(2*n-1) # but for gradient use Lg. Could use Lg for b too, larger but one L. Use L=next_pow2(3*n-2) for both.

B = irfft(P*P,L)[:2n-1]
M = B.max()
# compute weights W
Wref = W[::-1]
G = irfft(P * rfft(Wref,L),L)
g = 2*G[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
```

### Adam optimizer with target/hinge

```
def target_adam(parent, duration_s, rng, target_score=None, sigma_mode='hinge', ...):
    p=normalize(parent)
    n=p.size; L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    z=log(p) normalized
    m=v=zeros
    best = fast_score(p)
    phase=0
    no_improve=0
    step=0
    # choose hyperparams adaptively
    while time < deadline:
       if step % erase? no.
       P = np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       B = np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2n-1]
       B = np.maximum(B,0)
       M = float(B.max())
       score = 2*n*M
       if score < best - 1e-12: best=score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve += 1
       # if no_improve reset? We'll reset after N steps.
       target_M = target_score/(2n) if target_score else 0.0
       # choose T/sigma
       if no_improve > 3000:
           # randomize: mix with best and noise, reset momentum, lower lr?
       # weights
       if use_hinge:
           # T = max(target_M, M - hinge_frac*M)
           T = max(target_M, M*(1.0 - hinge_frac))
           # Ensure T < M
           if T >= M: T = 0.999999*M
           W = B - T
           W[W < 0] = 0
           W /= W.sum() if sum>0 else fallback
           # maybe emphasize top: W *= (B?)? no.
       else:
           sigma = sigma_ratio*M
           zsc = (B - M)/sigma
           W = exp(zsc); W/=sum
       g = gradient using P and W
       gz = g - p*np.dot(p,g)
       # maybe add small entropy/smoothing regularization? Could add alpha*p*(mean? )? If want push uniform: add -alpha*? We'll include if stagnant: gz += mix_rate * (uniform/n? - p?)? But might worsen. Maybe after many no improve.
       adam step
       p=softmax(z)
       step++
       if step%5000 print best
```

- `hinge_frac`: start 0.02 (top 2%? actually T = M -0.02M, weight above closest 2% of max. The fraction of lags above depends shape; maybe 1% of lags.) - `max_no_improve`: If no improvement for e.g. 5000 steps (score not decreasing), apply restart/local search:
   * Try smaller lr, different hinge_frac.
   * Mix current p with best and random noise from a random smooth parent or uniform.

### Could we derive a better explicit sequence using polynomial root/spectral? Let's explore more to maybe produce seed that beats 1.5.

Try to design p_i from target b_l flat. For large n, use truncated Fourier. Suppose we set b_l ≈ C for l=1..2n-2, and b_0=b_{2n-2}=0? But sum b=1 => (2n-2)C≈1 => C≈1/(2n). Then A(z)^2 ≈ C ∑_{l=1}^{2n-2} z^l = C z(1-z^{2n-1})/(1-z). Then A(z) ≈ sqrt(C) z^{1/2} sqrt((1-z^{2n-1})/(1-z)). Coefficients? For large n, the coefficient of z^{n-1}? Square root of (1-z^N)/(1-z)=1+...+z^{N-1}. The coefficients ~1/√l? Nonnegative? The square root of a finite geometric series with positive coefficients may have alternating signs? Let's check small: sqrt(1+z+z^2) = 1 + z/2 - z^2/8 ... negative. So A coefficients would alternate signs, not nonnegative. But perhaps with phase due z^{1/2} and boundary? Not. ### Restart strategies in target_adam
Implement in one function with periodic restart after `stall_steps`. Behavior:
- Keep `best_p`, `best_score`.
- Every `restart_period` (e.g. 8000 steps) or if no_improve > `no_improve_limit` (e.g. 6000):
   Generate a candidate from a mixture of `best_p` and a random smooth parent saved `parents_pool`, or noise on best.
   Choose its score; if lower than current? set p; reset momentum; maybe change parameters (hinge_frac, lr).
But random parents may be worse; maybe use small noise only. We can use three types:
   1. `p = normalize(best_p * (1 + rng.normal(0, 0.1)))` (multiplicative noise), then clip.
   2. `p = normalize((1-mix)*best_p + mix*uniform_parent)`
   3. Upsample a low-dim random parent to N and mix.
Set current p to it if its score < current_score + some tolerance? Even if worse, could escape; but we should not degrade too much. Use if no_improve high and candidate score < best_score + 0.05 maybe.

Since time limited, restarts few. Could also use simulated annealing: allow worse with probability exp(delta/T). But not necessary.

Multi-start outside: We can run target_adam on several parents sequentially with time slices. For each parent, initialize p, run, update global best. If one stalls, move to next. This may improve robust. But too many resets. Simpler: Run target_adam on best parent; inside it uses random restarts. Use parents pool for restarts.

### Pseudo-code target_adam
```
def target_adam(parent, deadline, rng, target_score=1.50, init_hinge=0.04, lr0=0.02, print_every=10000):
    p = resize/normalize parent
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(3*n-2)
    z = log(p); z -= mean; clip
    m = zeros(v)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score(p)
    no_improve=0
    streak=0
    step=0
    hinge=init_hinge
    lr=lr0
    momentum_coeff=1.0
    # Maybe initial candidates for restart: parents_pool? We can provide a callable? No closures. Pass a list parents.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if no_improve > 20000: # adjust
           hinge = min(0.25, hinge*1.3)
           if no_improve > 40000: restart...
       # compute
       P = rfft(p,L)
       B = irfft(P*P,L)[:2n-1]
       B = np.maximum(B,0)
       M = B.max()
       score = 2*n*M
       if score < best_score - 1e-10:
           best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0; streak+=1
           if streak %? maybe decrease hinge? or lr? 
           lr *= 1.0? keep
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           streak=0
       # If best_score <= target and no_improve large: try polish with smaller hinge and lower lr
       T = M * (1.0 - hinge)
       T = max(T, target_score/(2*n))
       if T >= M: T = M*(1-1e-6)
       excess = B - T
       pos = excess > 0
       if pos.any():
           W = np.where(pos, excess, 0)
           Wsq = W * B? maybe W**2? Use W (hinge)
           sw = W.sum()
           if sw>0: W/=sw
           else: W = softmax((B-M)/0.05*M)
       else: fallback
       Wref = W[::-1]
       G = irfft(P * rfft(Wref,L),L)
       g = 2*G[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       gz = g - p * np.dot(p,g)
       # optional scale gz max to 1? Adam handles but if zeros? Use max_g = max(abs(gz)); if finite and >0, don't clip except inf.
       step +=1
       m=...
       z -= lr*(m/b1)/(sqrt(v/b2)+eps)
       z = finite; z -= mean; clip
       p=softmax(z)
       # periodic no-improve restarts
       if no_improve > 8000 and step % restart_step ==0:
           # generate candidate, if good
       
    return best_p,best_score
```

Bytes: For target M, check if T below 0. If B all below T, fallback.

Should we normalize W by `sw` or by count? Gradient scale affects lr? Adam normalizes RMS, so scale not matter much. If W normalized sum=1, g ~ B scale 1/n; Adam RMS ~1/n, lr effective lr. If use power, similar. Use normalized.

Potential problem: If W only on few lags, g has large spikes? It is correlation of sparse W with p; values maybe sum weights * p ~1/n. RMS similar. Good.

### Potential algorithm using continuous differential equations

```
def fast_score(p):
   p=normalize_positive(p); if None inf
   n=p.size; L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   B=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
   B=np.maximum(B,0)
   M=float(B.max())
   return 2.0*n*M
```

```
def random_fourier_parent(n, rng, D=16):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    # random frequencies? Use m=1..D
    val = np.zeros(n)
    for m in 1..D:
       amp = rng.lognormal(-0.5, 0.8) / (m ** rng.uniform(0.3,1.2))
       phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi)
       # bias toward sine: if phase? Maybe use random cos/sin; phase random covers both.
       val += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
    # Optionally center x? For interval, boundaries. Nonnegative:
    mode = rng.integers(3)
    if mode==0:
       # rescale to keep in [-0.8,0.8]
       if np.max(np.abs(val))>0: val = val/np.max(np.abs(val))*0.8
       h = 1.0 + val
    elif mode==1:
       h = np.exp(np.clip(val, -3,3))
    else:
       # tanh
       h = 1.0 + np.tanh(val) # min between 0 and2? tanh ±1 => [0,2]
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-8)
    return normalize_positive(h)
```

### More mathematical improvement: Use L1 projection to enforce target directly
Once target reached? Suppose current M > target_M. We can attempt to solve linearized equations: For current p, let d = p_new - p small, sum d=0. Change db_l ≈ p_l? `db_l = ∑ (d_i p_{l-i}+p_i d_{l-i}) = 2(d * p)_l_rev?`. We want db_l ≤ target_M - b_l for lags above. This is a linear feasibility problem: find d in simplex tangent such that A d ≤ c. Could use linear programming! Dimensions n=8192, constraints 2n; LP maybe heavy but HiGHS could solve? Previous LP used constraints f*g≤M with g variable, not d. But maybe a small LP for d with active constraints and sum d=0,bounds? We could solve:
- Choose active lags where b_l > target_M (or near max). Constraints: A_row_l d ≤ c_l, where c_l = target_M - b_l (negative or positive? If b_l>target, c_l negative, need decrease). A_row_l relates to g_l/2: Arow_l d = (d_i p_{l-i}+p_i d_{l-i})? More precisely db_l=∑_i (d_i p_{l-i}+p_i d_{l-i}) = 2∑_i d_i p_{l-i} (linear). We want 2∑_i d_i p_{l-i} ≤ target_M - b_l = c_l (<0). Bounded |d_i| ≤ ε p_i? To keep positive. And ∑d=0. This is an LP! If feasible, take step d, update p. This could systematically reduce all targeted lags. def lp_local_step(p, b, target_score, K=256):
```
    n=p.size
    M=b.max(); target_M=target_score/(2*n)
    # select top K lags by b, maybe if all? If K< len, sort indices by b descending
    idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:] maybe (if K >= len all)
    idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]] # not necessary
    rows = np.empty((K,n+1))
    rhs = np.empty(K)
    for r,l in enumerate(idx):
       s = max(0, l-n+1); e=min(n-1,l)
       if e>=s:
          # t index from? v_i = 2*p[l-i]; i from s to e; p index j = l-i ranges l-s to l-e reversed
          vals = 2.0 * p[l-e:l-s+1][::-1]? Let's compute: i=s -> p_l? Example l=5,n=10, s=0,e=5; i=0 p5, i=1 p4,... i=5 p0. p[l-e:l-s+1] = p[0:6] = p0..p5; reversed = p5..p0. Good.
          # i=e -> p0.
          rows[r,s:e+1] = 2.0 * p[l-e:l-s+1][::-1]
       rows[r,n] = -1.0
       excess = max(0.0, b[l] - target_M)
       # To ensure if b below target, no constraint? If excess=0, constraint -t≤0 => t≥0, okay. But selected top mostly excess.
       rhs[r] = -excess
    c = zeros(n+1); c[n]=1
    A_eq = np.ones(n)
    bounds = [(lo_i,hi_i) for i in range(n)] + [(0,None)]
    res = linprog(c,A_ub=rows,b_ub=rhs,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=[0.0],bounds=bounds,method='highs')
```

```
def conv_linear(p):
```

```
def clean_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-12: return None
    return a/s
```
`normalize_positive` same.

```
def generate_parent_pool(n, count, deadline, rng, first_parents):
    parents=[]
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for idx in first_parents: parents.append((idx, fast_score(idx)))

    D=14

       mode=rng.integers(5)

             cp=cmath? use rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi); sp=np.sin(cp); cp_=np.cos(cp)
             val += amp*(sp*sin_table[m-1] + cp_*cos_table[m-1])
          h = 1.0 + 0.9*tanh(val)  # range [0.1,1.9]
          p=normalize_positive(h)

       elif mode==2: # Cheb basis with table? Need compute basis D maybe precompute.

       elif mode==3: # two gaussian anti

       s=fast_score(p)
       if s<... keep top 12 sorted
    return parents, best_parent, best_score
```

### Stage details with `compare_update`
`compare_update` should update by fast score. Since different lengths scores not directly comparable? Objective score comparable across lengths (lower better). Yes.

```
def compare_update(p, s, best_p, best_s):
   if p is None or not np.isfinite(s): return best_p,best_s
   if best_p is None or s < best_s:
       return p.copy(), float(s)
   return best_p, best_s
```
But if p length not same as desired, okay. For stage best, maybe want best at specific length. We track global best across all lengths. For parent selection for stage, maybe use best global.

### Potential issue: The objective value for a sequence length lower vs higher: A continuous shape may have lower score at higher n; but if we compare a high-n candidate and low-n candidate, okay because evaluator objective includes n. We should compare actual scores.

### When resizing best to larger N, score may change. Run target_adam from that resized candidate; its initial score maybe worse. But optimizer may improve.

### Use target_score variable maybe 1.499? If target 1.50 and target_M lower than feasible, T may be max target. Good. To bias, set `target_score = 1.499` to force any coefficient below 0.7495/n? If infeasible, no issue; T max with (1-hinge)M. When M near 1.5027, target 1.499 T=0.7495/n, all lags above slightly? Maybe too strict; but still weights. Use 1.500 as target. Maybe 1.5020. Let's set `TARGET = 1.500` (slightly below the 1.5027 threshold). For stage if not reached, still optimize.

### Testing mentally small code issues

### More about LP step sign and equality
LP objective minimize t. The constraint row with t=-1. If `excess` negative? We set excess=max(0,b-target). rhs=-excess (<=0). The row coefficient for d: `2*p[l-i]`. If p sum=1, row entries positive. For a high lag, to reduce b, we need choose d such that A d negative enough. Since p_{l-i} positive, can set d_i negative where p_{l-i} large, i.e., positions that paired with partners. Sum d=0 enforces positive d elsewhere. This may increase other lags. Feasible if within bounds. Good.

LP may pick t=0 and d reducing all selected to target. If target below achievable, optimum t>0. Good.

Potential issue: If selected lags include b below target (excess=0), constraints -t≤0, okay. But if all selected have excess=0 (M≤target), LP returns d=0,t=0. Good.

Line search after LP: If d very small, no improve. Use multiple `theta`. Note the LP d may already at bounds; theta=1 could negative if bounds not strict? We set lower=-0.25*p, upper=0.25*p. With theta=1 nonnegative. But due renormalization, if sum q maybe 1. Use `np.maximum(q,0)` to be safe.

### Time for LP at N=16384
Scipy linprog with dense 128x16385, 16k variables, 128 constraints +1 equality. HiGHS should be fast maybe 0.1-1s. For safety, K=96? Matrix 1.5M 12MB. Use K=128. Number of LPs maybe until no improve; limit deadline 90s. If each 2s, ~45 steps. Good.

### Could `linprog` with equality ones and bounds be slow due many variables? Variables with zero bounds? p all positive. Maybe okay. Use `options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance': ...}` not necessary.

### Need handle linprog result `res.x` may be None. If objective t close to current excess, d maybe zero. We can compute `dmax = np.max(np.abs(d))`; if <1e-14 skip.

### How to choose K in LP polish
Start K=128, if no improve for 3, increase K=192, 256, 384. At n=16k, 384*16k=6.3M 50MB, okay but solve slower. Max K=384. Maybe if n>12000, max K=192. Implement:

```
if n <= 4096: Kbase=256
elif n <= 8192: Kbase=192
else: Kbase=128
K = min(len(b), Kbase + 64*level)
```
`level` increases up to 4.

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
    return a / s
```
If all zeros, returns None. For parent generation, if None uniform.

`uniform_vec(n)`:
```
def uniform_vec(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: n=1
    return np.full(n, 1.0/float(n), dtype=np.float64)
```

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p=normalize_positive(seq)
    m=int(m)
    if m<=0: m=1
    if p is None: return uniform_vec(m)
    n=p.size
    if n==m: return p.copy()
    if n==1 or m==1: return uniform_vec(m)
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n); xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    out=np.interp(xn,xo,p)
    return normalize_positive(out)
```

`softmax`:
```
def softmax_vec(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z=z-z.max()
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return uniform_vec(e.size)
    return e/s
```

`conv_linear`:
```
def conv_linear(p, L=None):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if L is None: L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    B=np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
    B=np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    B=np.maximum(B,0.0)
    return B
```
If p has zeros and sum? okay.

`score_from_conv`:
```
def score_from_conv(p,B):
    n=p.size
    if B.size==0: return np.inf
    M=float(np.max(B))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M<0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M)
```
`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(p):
   q=normalize_positive(p)
   if q is None: return np.inf
   B=conv_linear(q)
   return score_from_conv(q,B)
```

`compare_update`: as above.

```
def cheb_basis_matrix(n,D):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    t=np.clip(2*x-1,-1,1)
    ang=np.arccos(t)
    B=np.empty((n,D),float)
    for m in range(1,D+1): B[:,m-1]=np.cos(m*ang)
    return B
```
Maybe D=12.

```
def fourier_parent_from_vals(vals, mode, rng, n, level=0.8):
   if mode==0:
       h = 1.0 + level*np.tanh(vals)
   elif mode==1:
       h = np.exp(np.clip(vals,-3,3))
   else:

```

```
def cheb_parent(B, rng, D, mode):
   theta = rng.normal(0,0.5,D)
   theta[0] = rng.choice([-1,1]) * rng.uniform(0.3,1.2)
   if D>3: theta[2:]*=0.6
   val = B.dot(theta)
   if mode==0: h=1.1 + 0.7*val/max(1,abs max)
   else: h=np.exp(clip(val,-3,3))
   return normalize_positive(h)
```

```
def gauss_parent(n,rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   c1=rng.uniform(0.2,0.45); c2=1.0-c1+rng.uniform(-0.08,0.08)
   sigma=rng.lognormal(-1.6,0.5)
   g1=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/sigma)**2)
   g2=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/sigma)**2)
   a=rng.uniform(0.4,1.4)
   h=1.0 + a*g1 - a*g2
   # optionally add small second hump to keep support
   h += 0.1*np.random? no
   return normalize_positive(np.maximum(h,1e-10))
```
This creates anti shape high near c1, low near c2.

### LP step function top-level
Need pass `p, B, target_score, K`. Let's write carefully.
```
def lp_local_step(p, B, target_score, K, max_bound_frac=0.25):
    if not HAVE_LINPROG: return None, np.inf
    n=p.size
    m=len(B)
    if K <=0: return None,np.inf
    K = min(K, m)
    # select indices by partition
    if K < m:
       idx = np.argpartition(B, -K)[-K:]
    else:
       idx = np.arange(m)
    # order not important
    target_M = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
    rows = np.zeros((K, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
    rhs = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    p_safe = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    for r, l in enumerate(idx):
       l=int(l)
       s = max(0, l - n + 1)
       e = min(n - 1, l)
       if e >= s:
           # p indices from l-e to l-s inclusive
           seg = p_safe[l-e:l-s+1]
           rows[r, s:e+1] = 2.0 * seg[::-1]
       rows[r, n] = -1.0
       excess = float(B[l]) - target_M
       if excess < 0.0: excess = 0.0
       if not np.isfinite(excess): excess=0.0
       rhs[r] = -excess
    c = np.zeros(n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0
    A_eq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_eq = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    lower = -max_bound_frac * np.maximum(p_safe, 1e-14)
    upper = max_bound_frac * np.maximum(p_safe, 1e-14)
    # To allow sum equality, if all lower/upper zero? p positive. fine.
    bounds = [(float(lower[i]), float(upper[i])) for i in range(n)]
    bounds.append((0.0, None))
    try:
       res = linprog(c, A_ub=rows, b_ub=rhs, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       return None, np.inf
    if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False) or getattr(res,'x',None) is None:
       return None, np.inf
    d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) <= 1e-14:
       return None, np.inf
    return d, float(res.x[n]) if np.isfinite(res.x[n]) else 0.0
```
Potential num rows iteration in Python K=256; fine. `rows[r, s:e+1] = 2*seg[::-1]`: `seg` already in order p[l-e]..p[l-s]; reversed gives p[l-s]..p[l-e]? Let's verify. Example n=5,l=3. s=max(0,-1)=0, e=min(4,3)=3. Valid i=0..3. p index l-i: 3,2,1,0. `seg = p[l-e:l-s+1] = p[0:4] = p0,p1,p2,p3`. `seg[::-1]=p3,p2,p1,p0`. assign to i0..3 correct.
Example l=7,n=5: s=max(0,3)=3,e=min4,7=4; valid i=3,4: p index 4,3. seg=p[l-e=3:l-s+1=5]=p3,p4; reversed p4,p3. correct.

Line search in `lp_polish`:

```
def lp_polish(parent, deadline, target_score, name, max_no_improve=4):

       current=score_from_conv(p,B)
       if current < best_score - ... update? though p is best? We update if.
       if best_score <= target_score + 1e-7: # maybe continue to polish? break? Could continue for a bit to lower target? target still 1.5; if below no further need? but maybe lower to 1.49? We can continue with target_score lower? Not needed; LP may not improve. Break maybe.
       if n <=4096: base=256
       elif n<=8192: base=192

       K = min(2*n-1, base + 48*level)
```

### target_adam function more code
Let's write fully.
```
def target_adam(parent, duration_s, rng, parents, target_score=1.50, name="adam", start_hinge=0.04, start_lr=0.02, print_every=20000):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None: return uniform_vec(4096?), inf
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0,float(duration_s))
    hinge=start_hinge; lr=start_lr
    no_improve=0; step=0; restarts=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # If no_improve too high, maybe skip? handle below after step.
       P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
       B = np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
       B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
       B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
       M = float(B.max()) if B.size else np.inf
       score = float(2.0*n*M) if np.isfinite(M) else np.inf
       if np.isfinite(score):
           if score < best_score - 1e-11:
               best_score = score; best_p = p.copy(); no_improve = 0
           else:
               no_improve += 1
       # If B not finite break
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M <=0: break
       T = M * (1.0 - hinge)
       T = max(T, target_score / (2.0*n))
       if T >= M: T = M * 0.999999
       excess = B - T
       W = np.where(excess > 0.0, excess, 0.0)
       sw = float(W.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
           sigma = max(1e-12, 0.02*M)
           zsc = (B - M) / sigma
           W = np.exp(np.clip(zsc, -50.0, 0.0))
           sw = float(W.sum())
           if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0:
              W = np.zeros_like(B); W[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0; sw=1.0
       W = W / sw
       Wref = W[::-1]
       G = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
       g = 2.0 * G[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
       mean_pg = float(np.dot(p, g))
       gz = g - p * mean_pg
       if no_improve > 12000:
          # small pull to uniform
          gz = gz + 1e-5 * z
       max_g = float(np.max(np.abs(gz)))
       if not np.isfinite(max_g) or max_g <= 1e-15:
          # restart maybe
       step +=1
       m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*gz
       v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(gz*gz)
       b1 = 1 - beta1**step
       b2 = 1 - beta2**step
       z = z - lr * (m/b1) / (np.sqrt(v/b2) + eps)
       z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
       z -= z.mean()
       z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
       p = softmax_vec(z)
       # adaptive
       if no_improve > 4000 and step % 1000 ==0:
          hinge = min(0.35, hinge*1.25)
       if no_improve > 12000 and step % 2000 ==0:
          lr = max(0.002, lr*0.8)
       if best_score < target_score + 0.005 and step % 3000 ==0:
          hinge = max(0.01, hinge*0.8)
          lr = max(0.003, lr*0.9)
       # restart condition
       if (no_improve > 25000 or step % 100000 == 0) and restarts < 8:
          cand, cs = make_restart(p, best_p, parents, rng, n, target_score, best_score)
          if cand is not None:
             p = cand.copy()
             z = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z = where finite; z-=mean; clip
             m *=0; v*=0
             no_improve=0
             lr = max(0.005, lr*0.8)
             hinge = min(0.15, max(0.03, hinge*1.2))
             restarts +=1
       if step % print_every ==0:
          print(name, "step", step, "best", best_score, "current", score)
    return best_p, best_score
```
Need if `max_g` finite? If gz zero, update no change. fine.

`make_restart` top-level:
```
def make_restart(current, best, parents, rng, n, target_score, best_score):
    candidates = []
    # current noise
    if current is not None and current.size == n:
        noise = current * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n))
        candidates.append(noise)
        lin = (1-0.2)*best + 0.2*uniform_vec(n)
        candidates.append(lin)
    if parents:
       for _ in range(3):
          par = parents[int(rng.integers(len(parents)))]
          parN = resize_sequence(par, n)
          if parN is None: parN = uniform_vec(n)
          t = float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.7))
          lin = (1-t)*best + t*parN
          candidates.append(lin)
          # log mix
          za = np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
          zb = np.log(np.maximum(parN,1e-300))
          zmix = (1-t)*za + t*zb
          candidates.append(softmax_vec(zmix))
    # select best among candidates if it is not too worse
    selected=None; sel_s=best_score + 0.05
    for c in candidates:
       pc=normalize_positive(c)
       if pc is None or pc.size != n: continue
       s=fast_score(pc)
       if np.isfinite(s) and s < sel_s:
          sel_s=s; selected=pc
    return selected, sel_s if selected is not None else np.inf
```
This uses `best` of same length n? In target_adam, best_p length n. `parents` are length n_scan; resize. Good. If `selected` worse than best by <0.05, it will be chosen; could be slightly worse to escape. If all worse by >0.05, none; condition? We set sel_s=best_score+0.05, so if no candidate < current+0.05, return None. If current (noise) maybe. If best_score itself +? fine.
But if selected is worse than best, we still use to escape. In target_adam, no_improve resets; later if not improve, can restart again. Good.

`make_restart` calls fast_score up to 7 times; expensive but rare. Could limit to 4 candidates. We'll set 5.

Potential issue: adding z pull `gz += 1e-5*z` when z large maybe alters; okay.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    if 'budget' in kwargs: budget_s=kwargs['budget']? maybe.
    raw_budget = max(0.0, min(float(budget_s), 2380.0))

    if raw_budget < 300:
       N1=4096; N2=4096
    elif raw_budget < 1000:
       N1=8192; N2=8192
    else:
       N1=8192; N2=16384
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # load previous
    prev = None
    try:
       prev_raw = height_sequence_1  # global
    except NameError:

       prev = normalize_positive(prev_raw)

       # maybe also resized to N1? fast_score at its length; update with N1 if score better

    # ensure at least uniform at N1

       compare

       parent1 = resize_sequence(best_p, N1)

       d1 = max(60, dur1)
       p1,s1 = target_adam(parent1, d1, rng, parents, target_score=1.50, name="adam1", start_hinge=0.04, start_lr=0.02)
       compare p1,s1

           lp_dead = min(deadline-120, time+120)

           compare pl,sl
    # Stage 2 at N2

       parent2 = resize_sequence(best_p, N2)
       if time < deadline - 30:
          # maybe run LP initial? no
          dur2 = min(deadline - time - 30, max(120.0, raw_budget*0.45))
          p2,s2 = target_adam(parent2, dur2, rng, parents, target_score=1.50, name="adam2", start_hinge=0.06, start_lr=0.018)
          compare
       if HAVE_LINPROG and best_p is not None and best_p.size == N2 and time < deadline - 20:
          lp_dead = min(deadline-20, time+120)
          pl,sl=lp_polish(resize_sequence(best_p,N2), lp_dead, 1.50, name="lp2")
          compare
    # If remaining >120, do another polish on best of its own length with different hinger
    if time < deadline - 120:
       pcur = best_p
       ncur = pcur.size
       dur3 = deadline - time - 30
       # maybe if ncur==N2 and enough time, run target_adam again from best with start_hinge=0.1, lr=0.01 for min(600,dur3)
       if dur3 > 120:
          p3,s3=target_adam(pcur, dur3, rng, parents, target_score=1.50, name="adam3", start_hinge=0.10, start_lr=0.012)
          compare
       if HAVE_LINPROG and time < deadline - 30 and best_p is not None:
          lp_dead = min(deadline - 20, time+90)
          pl,sl=lp_polish(best_p, lp_dead, 1.50, name="lp3")
          compare
    # Final sequence from best_p
    final_seq = to_list(best_p)
    return final_seq
```

### Let's define `add_to_top`:
```
def add_to_top(p, s, top_ps, top_s, cap=10):
    if p is None or not np.isfinite(s):
        return top_ps, top_s
    top_ps.append(p)
    top_s.append(float(s))
    if len(top_ps) > cap:
        # remove the worst (largest) score
        idx = int(np.argmax(np.asarray(top_s, dtype=np.float64)))
        top_ps.pop(idx)
        top_s.pop(idx)
    return top_ps, top_s
```
If `top_s` changes, okay. It may remove initial if worse. Good.

#### `generate_parent_pool` details
Inside, use `time.monotonic` (import time). Use `initial_parents` list. Need if initial_parents None.
```
def generate_parent_pool(n, count, deadline, rng, initial_parents, max_cap=12):
    top_ps=[]; top_s=[]
    if initial_parents:
       for p0 in initial_parents:
          p=resize_sequence(p0,n)
          if p is not None:
             s=fast_score(p)
             top_ps,top_s=add_to_top(p,s,top_ps,top_s,max_cap)
    x=...
    Df=12; sin_tabs=[]; cos_tabs=[]
    for m in range(1,Df+1): ...
    Dc=8
    B=cheb_basis_matrix(n,Dc)
    # deterministic:
    for amp in (0.6,0.8,1.0):
        p=normalize_positive(1.0+amp*np.sin(2πx))
        s=fast_score(p); add
    p=normalize_positive(1.0+0.6*sin1+0.3*sin2)
    ...
    count=int(count)
    for ii in range(count):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       mode=int(rng.integers(5))
       p=None
       if mode==0 or mode==1:
          val=np.zeros(n)
          # random amps array loop
          for m in range(1,Df+1):
             amp=float(rng.lognormal(-0.7,0.7)) / (float(m)**0.5)
             phase=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
             sp=float(np.sin(phase)); cp=float(np.cos(phase))
             val += amp * (sp * sin_tabs[m-1] + cp * cos_tabs[m-1])
          if mode==0:
             # random size level
             level=float(rng.uniform(0.55,1.1))
             p=normalize_positive(1.0 + level*np.tanh(val))
          else:
             p=normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(val, -3.0, 3.0)))
       elif mode==2:
          theta = rng.normal(0.0,0.5,Dc)
          if Dc>0:
             theta[0] = (1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0)*float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.2))
          if Dc>3: theta[2:]*=0.6
          val=B.dot(theta)
          mad=float(np.max(np.abs(val)))
          if mad>0: val=val/mad
          p=normalize_positive(1.0 + 0.9*np.tanh(val))
       elif mode==3:
          p=gauss_parent(n,rng)
       else:
          # mixture: exp of sum of two gaussian? or higher freq
          val=np.zeros(n)
          for m in (2,3,5,7,11):
             amp = float(rng.normal(0.5,0.4))
             phase = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
             sp=... cp=...
             val += amp*(sp*sin_tabs[m-1]+cp*cos_tabs[m-1])
          p=normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(val,-3,3)))
       if p is None: continue
       s=fast_score(p)
       top_ps,top_s=add_to_top(p,s,top_ps,top_s,max_cap)
       if (ii+1)%250==0:
          cur_best = float(min(top_s)) if top_s else np.inf
          print("parent pool", n, ii+1, "best", cur_best)
    best_p = top_ps[int(np.argmin(top_s))] if top_ps else None
    best_s = float(min(top_s)) if top_ps else np.inf
    return top_ps, best_p, best_s
```
Need if mode==4 uses sin_tabs[m-1] for m=11; Df=12 so okay. If m=2,3,5,7,11 available. Good.

`gauss_parent`: Use rng.normal? We can add small random smooth noise:
```
def gauss_parent(n,rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   c1=rng.uniform(0.2,0.45); c2=1-c1+rng.uniform(-0.1,0.1)
   if c2<0.05 or c2>0.95: c2=1-c1
   sigma=rng.lognormal(-1.7,0.5)
   g1=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/sigma)**2)
   g2=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/sigma)**2)
   a=rng.uniform(0.4,1.5)
   h=1.0 + a*g1 - a*g2
   # Add tiny full support random? maybe
   return normalize_positive(np.maximum(h,1e-10))
```
This h min maybe negative if a large and g2=1: 1 -1.5 = -0.5; clipped to 1e-10, okay. It may produce zero around c2 if a>1, reducing support; maybe okay. Use a max1.2? `a=rng.uniform(0.4,1.2)` to keep min>=-0.2. Actually g1 max1, g2 max1; if c1,c2 overlap? min could 1-a(g1-g2). If a=1.5 negative. Use a=1.0 maybe. Set a=rng.uniform(0.35,1.1). Clip.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    try:
       raw_budget=float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
       raw_budget=2380.0
    if 'budget_s' in kwargs: ... not necessary.
    raw_budget=max(0.0, min(raw_budget, 2380.0))
    deadline = start_time + max(0.0, raw_budget - 45.0)
    if raw_budget < 300: N1=4096; N2=4096
    elif raw_budget < 1100: N1=8192; N2=8192
    else: N1=8192; N2=16384
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    prev=None
    try:
       prev=normalize_positive(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
       prev=None
    best_p=None; best_s=np.inf
    if prev is not None:
       s=fast_score(prev); best_p,best_s=compare_update(prev,s,best_p,best_s)
       for N in (N1,N2):
          if N != prev.size:
             pr=resize_sequence(prev,N); sr=fast_score(pr); best_p,best_s=compare_update(pr,sr,best_p,best_s)
    # uniform
    u1=uniform_vec(N1); s=fast_score(u1); compare
    print("initial", best_s)
    # parent pool
    n_scan = 4096 if N1 >=8192 else N1
    parent_deadline = min(deadline, start_time + max(30.0, min(120.0, raw_budget*0.05)))
    count = 900 if raw_budget > 1500 else (500 if raw_budget > 800 else 150)
    initials = [prev, best_p]
    parents, parent_best, parent_score = generate_parent_pool(n_scan, count, parent_deadline, rng, initials)
    for par in parents:
       s=fast_score(par); best_p,best_s=compare_update(par,s,best_p,best_s)
    print("after parents", best_s)
```

```python
"""
Hybrid nonnegative step-function search.

The objective is scale-invariant and equivalent to minimizing
2*n*max_k (a*a)_k over a>=0.  This module searches for sequences with low
linear convolution peaks by combining:
  - FFT-based exact fast evaluation,
  - low-dimensional smooth parent generation (Fourier/Chebyshev/Gaussian),
  - top-violation smoothed Adam in log-probability coordinates,
  - a linearized local LP polishing step,
  - continuation from smaller to larger lengths.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    HAVE_LINPROG = False


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def uniform_vec(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p = normalize_positive(seq)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        m = 1
    if p is None:
        return uniform_vec(m)
    n = p.size
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    if n == 1 or m == 1:
        return uniform_vec(m)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    return normalize_positive(out)


def softmax_vec(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return uniform_vec(e.size)
    return e / s


def conv_linear(p, L=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    return B


def score_from_conv(p, B):
    n = p.size
    if B.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    M = float(np.max(B))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def fast_score(p):
    q = normalize_positive(p)
    if q is None:
        return np.inf
    B = conv_linear(q)
    return score_from_conv(q, B)


def compare_update(p, s, best_p, best_s):
    if p is None or not np.isfinite(s):
        return best_p, best_s
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if best_p is None or s < best_s:
        return p.copy(), float(s)
    return best_p, float(best_s)


def cheb_basis_matrix(n, D):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    t = np.clip(2.0 * x - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)
    ang = np.arccos(t)
    B = np.empty((n, D), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, D + 1):
        B[:, m - 1] = np.cos(m * ang)
    return B


def gauss_parent(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.45))
    c2 = 1.0 - c1 + float(rng.uniform(-0.10, 0.10))
    if c2 < 0.05 or c2 > 0.95:
        c2 = 1.0 - c1
    sigma = float(rng.lognormal(-1.7, 0.5))
    g1 = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c1) / sigma) ** 2)
    g2 = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c2) / sigma) ** 2)
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.10))
    h = 1.0 + amp * g1 - amp * g2
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-10)
    return normalize_positive(h)


def add_to_top(p, s, top_ps, top_s, cap=10):
    if p is None or not np.isfinite(s):
        return top_ps, top_s
    top_ps.append(p)
    top_s.append(float(s))
    if len(top_ps) > cap:
        idx = int(np.argmax(np.asarray(top_s, dtype=np.float64)))
        top_ps.pop(idx)
        top_s.pop(idx)
    return top_ps, top_s


def generate_parent_pool(n, count, deadline, rng, initial_parents, max_cap=12):
    top_ps = []
    top_s = []

    if initial_parents:
        for p0 in initial_parents:
            p = resize_sequence(p0, n)
            if p is not None:
                s = fast_score(p)
                top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, s, top_ps, top_s, max_cap)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Guaranteed nonnegative anti-symmetric / mixed parents.
    for amp in (0.55, 0.75, 0.95):
        p = normalize_positive(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))
        s = fast_score(p)
        top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, s, top_ps, top_s, max_cap)

    ang1 = 2.0 * np.pi * x
    ang2 = 4.0 * np.pi * x
    p = normalize_positive(1.0 + 0.60 * np.sin(ang1) + 0.28 * np.sin(ang2))
    top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, fast_score(p), top_ps, top_s, max_cap)
    p = normalize_positive(1.0 + 0.60 * np.sin(ang1) - 0.28 * np.sin(ang2))
    top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, fast_score(p), top_ps, top_s, max_cap)
    p = normalize_positive(1.0 + 0.70 * np.cos(ang1))
    top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, fast_score(p), top_ps, top_s, max_cap)
    p = normalize_positive(1.0 - 0.70 * np.cos(ang1))
    top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, fast_score(p), top_ps, top_s, max_cap)

    Df = 12
    sin_tabs = []
    cos_tabs = []
    for m in range(1, Df + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * x
        sin_tabs.append(np.sin(ang))
        cos_tabs.append(np.cos(ang))

    Dc = 8
    B = cheb_basis_matrix(n, Dc)

    count = int(count)
    for ii in range(count):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        mode = int(rng.integers(5))
        p = None

        if mode == 0 or mode == 1:
            val = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for m in range(1, Df + 1):
                amp = float(rng.lognormal(-0.70, 0.70)) / (float(m) ** 0.55)
                phase = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
                sp = float(np.sin(phase))
                cp = float(np.cos(phase))
                val += amp * (sp * sin_tabs[m - 1] + cp * cos_tabs[m - 1])
            if mode == 0:
                level = float(rng.uniform(0.55, 1.10))
                p = normalize_positive(1.0 + level * np.tanh(val))
            else:
                p = normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(val, -3.0, 3.0)))

        elif mode == 2:
            theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, Dc)
            if Dc > 0:
                theta[0] = (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0) * float(rng.uniform(0.30, 1.20))
            if Dc > 3:
                theta[2:] *= 0.6
            val = B.dot(theta)
            mad = float(np.max(np.abs(val)))
            if mad > 0.0:
                val = val / mad
            p = normalize_positive(1.0 + 0.90 * np.tanh(val))

        elif mode == 3:
            p = gauss_parent(n, rng)

        else:
            val = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for m in (2, 3, 5, 7, 11):
                amp = float(rng.normal(0.5, 0.45))
                phase = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
                sp = float(np.sin(phase))
                cp = float(np.cos(phase))
                val += amp * (sp * sin_tabs[m - 1] + cp * cos_tabs[m - 1])
            p = normalize_positive(np.exp(np.clip(val, -3.0, 3.0)))

        if p is None:
            continue
        s = fast_score(p)
        top_ps, top_s = add_to_top(p, s, top_ps, top_s, max_cap)

        if (ii + 1) % 250 == 0:
            cur = float(min(top_s)) if top_s else np.inf
            print(f"parent pool n={n} iters={ii+1} best={cur:.6f}")

    if not top_ps:
        return [uniform_vec(n)], uniform_vec(n), 2.0

    best_idx = int(np.argmin(np.asarray(top_s, dtype=np.float64)))
    return top_ps, top_ps[best_idx], float(top_s[best_idx])


def lp_local_step(p, B, target_score, K, max_bound_frac=0.25):
    if not HAVE_LINPROG:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    m = B.size
    K = int(min(max(1, K), m))

    p_safe = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    target_M = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))

    if K < m:
        idx = np.argpartition(B, -K)[-K:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(m)

    rows = np.zeros((K, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    rhs = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    for r, lag in enumerate(idx):
        lag = int(lag)
        s = max(0, lag - n + 1)
        e = min(n - 1, lag)
        if e >= s:
            seg = p_safe[lag - e:lag - s + 1]
            rows[r, s:e + 1] = 2.0 * seg[::-1]
        rows[r, n] = -1.0
        excess = float(B[lag]) - target_M
        if excess < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(excess):
            excess = 0.0
        rhs[r] = -excess

    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0

    A_eq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_eq = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    lower = -max_bound_frac * np.maximum(p_safe, 1e-14)
    upper = max_bound_frac * np.maximum(p_safe, 1e-14)
    bounds = [(float(lower[i]), float(upper[i])) for i in range(n)]
    bounds.append((0.0, None))

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=rows,
            b_ub=rhs,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"disp": False}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, np.inf

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None, np.inf

    d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) <= 1e-15:
        return None, np.inf

    t_opt = float(res.x[n]) if np.isfinite(res.x[n]) else 0.0
    return d, t_opt


def lp_polish(parent, deadline, target_score, name="lp", max_no_improve=3):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None:
        p = uniform_vec(4096)
    n = p.size

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(p)
    no_improve = 0
    level = 0
    steps = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and steps < 80:
        B = conv_linear(p)
        current = score_from_conv(p, B)
        if np.isfinite(current) and current < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_score = float(current)
            best_p = p.copy()

        if best_score <= float(target_score) + 1e-9:
            break

        if n <= 4096:
            base_K = 256
        elif n <= 8192:
            base_K = 192
        else:
            base_K = 112
        K = min(2 * n - 1, base_K + 40 * level)

        d, _ = lp_local_step(p, B, target_score, K, 0.25)
        cand = None
        cand_score = np.inf

        if d is not None:
            for theta in (0.80, 0.50, 0.25, 0.12, 0.05):
                q = p + theta * d
                q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
                s = float(q.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                q = q / s
                sc = fast_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                    cand = q
                    cand_score = float(sc)
                    break

        if cand is not None:
            p = cand
            best_p = cand.copy()
            best_score = cand_score
            no_improve = 0
            level = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            level += 1
            if no_improve >= max_no_improve:
                break

        steps += 1
        if steps % 4 == 0:
            print(f"{name} lp step {steps} best {best_score:.6f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def make_restart(current, best, parents, rng, n, target_score, best_score):
    candidates = []

    if current is not None and current.size == n:
        noise = current * np.exp(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n), -5.0, 5.0))
        candidates.append(noise)
        candidates.append(0.80 * best + 0.20 * uniform_vec(n))

    if parents:
        for _ in range(3):
            par = parents[int(rng.integers(len(parents)))]
            parN = resize_sequence(par, n)
            if parN is None:
                parN = uniform_vec(n)
            t = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.70))
            candidates.append((1.0 - t) * best + t * parN)

            za = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            zb = np.log(np.maximum(parN, 1e-300))
            candidates.append(softmax_vec((1.0 - t) * za + t * zb))

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        sel_s = np.inf
    else:
        allow = 0.03 if best_score < target_score + 0.01 else 0.07
        sel_s = best_score + allow

    selected = None
    selected_score = sel_s
    for c in candidates:
        pc = normalize_positive(c)
        if pc is None or pc.size != n:
            continue
        s = fast_score(pc)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < selected_score:
            selected_score = float(s)
            selected = pc

    if selected is None:
        return None, np.inf
    return selected, selected_score


def target_adam(parent, duration_s, rng, parents,
                target_score=1.50, name="adam",
                start_hinge=None, start_lr=None, print_every=20000):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None:
        p = uniform_vec(1024)
    n = p.size

    L = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z -= float(z.mean())
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(fast_score(p))

    if start_hinge is None:
        start_hinge = 0.045 if n <= 8192 else 0.065
    if start_lr is None:
        start_lr = 0.025 if n <= 8192 else 0.020

    hinge = float(start_hinge)
    lr = float(start_lr)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(duration_s))
    no_improve = 0
    step = 0
    restarts = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        B = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)

        M = float(B.max()) if B.size else np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        score = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-11:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        T = M * (1.0 - hinge)
        T = max(T, float(target_score) / (2.0 * n))
        if T >= M:
            T = M * 0.999999

        excess = B - T
        W = np.where(excess > 0.0, excess, 0.0)
        sw = float(W.sum())

        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
            sigma = max(1e-12, 0.02 * M)
            zsc = (B - M) / sigma
            W = np.exp(np.clip(zsc, -50.0, 0.0))
            sw = float(W.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
                W = np.zeros_like(B)
                W[int(np.argmax(B))] = 1.0
                sw = 1.0

        W = W / sw

        Wref = W[::-1]
        G = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
        g = 2.0 * G[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

        mean_pg = float(np.dot(p, g))
        gz = g - p * mean_pg
        if no_improve > 12000:
            gz = gz + 1e-5 * z

        max_g = float(np.max(np.abs(gz)))
        if not np.isfinite(max_g):
            gz = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        step += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * gz
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (gz * gz)
        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** step
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** step

        z = z - lr * (m / b1) / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps)
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z -= float(z.mean())
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
        p = softmax_vec(z)

        if best_score < float(target_score) + 0.005:
            if step % 3000 == 0:
                hinge = max(0.01, hinge * 0.80)
                lr = max(0.003, lr * 0.90)
        elif no_improve > 5000 and step % 1000 == 0:
            hinge = min(0.35, hinge * 1.25)

        if no_improve > 12000 and step % 2000 == 0:
            lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.80)

        if (no_improve > 25000 and restarts < 8
                and best_score > float(target_score) + 0.001
                and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0):
            cand, cs = make_restart(p, best_p, parents, rng, n, target_score, best_score)
            if cand is not None:
                p = cand.copy()
                z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
                z -= float(z.mean())
                z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                no_improve = 0
                lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.80)
                hinge = min(0.18, max(0.03, hinge * 1.20))
                restarts += 1

        if step % max(1, int(print_every)) == 0:
            print(f"{name} step {step} current {score:.6f} best {best_score:.6f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()

    raw_budget = budget_s
    for key in ("budget_s", "budget", "time_s", "time_limit_s", "run_time_s"):
        if key in kwargs:
            raw_budget = kwargs[key]
            break

    try:
        raw_budget = float(raw_budget)
    except Exception:
        raw_budget = 2380.0

    raw_budget = max(0.0, min(raw_budget, 2380.0))
    deadline = start_time + max(0.0, raw_budget - 45.0)

    if raw_budget < 300.0:
        N1 = 4096
        N2 = 4096
    elif raw_budget < 1100.0:
        N1 = 8192
        N2 = 8192
    else:
        N1 = 8192
        N2 = 16384

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    target_score = 1.500

    prev = None
    try:
        prev = normalize_positive(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf

    if prev is not None:
        s = fast_score(prev)
        best_p, best_s = compare_update(prev, s, best_p, best_s)
        for N in (N1, N2):
            if N != prev.size:
                pr = resize_sequence(prev, N)
                sr = fast_score(pr)
                best_p, best_s = compare_update(pr, sr, best_p, best_s)

    u1 = uniform_vec(N1)
    s1 = fast_score(u1)
    best_p, best_s = compare_update(u1, s1, best_p, best_s)
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = u1
        best_s = 2.0

    print(f"initial best {best_s:.6f} length {best_p.size}")

    n_scan = 4096 if N1 >= 8192 else N1
    parent_deadline = min(deadline, start_time + max(30.0, min(120.0, raw_budget * 0.05)))
    count = 900 if raw_budget > 1500.0 else (500 if raw_budget > 800.0 else 150)
    initials = [prev, best_p]
    parents, parent_best, parent_score = generate_parent_pool(
        n_scan, count, parent_deadline, rng, initials, max_cap=14
    )
    for par in parents:
        s = fast_score(par)
        best_p, best_s = compare_update(par, s, best_p, best_s)
    print(f"after parent pool best {best_s:.6f}")

    # ---------- Stage 1: moderate length ----------
    n1_best_p = None
    n1_best_s = np.inf

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if N2 != N1:
        dur1 = min(max(60.0, 0.35 * remaining), 1000.0)
    else:
        dur1 = max(60.0, remaining - 120.0)

    if dur1 > 45.0:
        parent1 = resize_sequence(best_p, N1)
        p1, s1 = target_adam(
            parent1, dur1, rng, parents,
            target_score=target_score,
            name="adam1",
            start_hinge=0.045,
            start_lr=0.025
        )
        n1_best_p, n1_best_s = compare_update(p1, s1, n1_best_p, n1_best_s)
        best_p, best_s = compare_update(n1_best_p, n1_best_s, best_p, best_s)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    reserve = 180.0 if N2 != N1 else 90.0
    if HAVE_LINPROG and n1_best_p is not None and rem > reserve:
        lp_dead = min(deadline - (60.0 if N2 != N1 else 30.0), time.monotonic() + 120.0)
        pl, sl = lp_polish(n1_best_p, lp_dead, target_score, name="lp1")
        n1_best_p, n1_best_s = compare_update(pl, sl, n1_best_p, n1_best_s)
        best_p, best_s = compare_update(n1_best_p, n1_best_s, best_p, best_s)

    print(f"after stage 1 best {best_s:.6f}")

    # ---------- Stage 2: larger length ----------
    n2_best_p = None
    n2_best_s = np.inf

    if N2 != N1:
        parent2 = resize_sequence(best_p, N2)
        s2_init = fast_score(parent2)
        n2_best_p, n2_best_s = compare_update(parent2, s2_init, n2_best_p, n2_best_s)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 200.0:
            dur2 = max(60.0, min(rem - 220.0, 1500.0))
            p2, s2 = target_adam(
                parent2, dur2, rng, parents,
                target_score=target_score,
                name="adam2",
                start_hinge=0.065,
                start_lr=0.020
            )
            n2_best_p, n2_best_s = compare_update(p2, s2, n2_best_p, n2_best_s)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if HAVE_LINPROG and n2_best_p is not None and rem > 120.0:
            lp_dead = min(deadline - 40.0, time.monotonic() + max(80.0, rem * 0.5))
            pl, sl = lp_polish(n2_best_p, lp_dead, target_score, name="lp2")
            n2_best_p, n2_best_s = compare_update(pl, sl, n2_best_p, n2_best_s)

        best_p, best_s = compare_update(n2_best_p, n2_best_s, best_p, best_s)
        print(f"after stage 2 best {best_s:.6f}")

    # ---------- Stage 3: final polish ----------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0 and best_p is not None:
        dur3 = max(60.0, min(rem - 100.0, 900.0))
        p3, s3 = target_adam(
            best_p, dur3, rng, parents,
            target_score=target_score,
            name="adam3",
            start_hinge=0.10,
            start_lr=0.012
        )
        best_p, best_s = compare_update(p3, s3, best_p, best_s)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if HAVE_LINPROG and best_p is not None and rem > 100.0:
        lp_dead = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, rem * 0.5))
        pl, sl = lp_polish(best_p, lp_dead, target_score, name="lp3")
        best_p, best_s = compare_update(pl, sl, best_p, best_s)

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = uniform_vec(1024)
        best_s = 2.0

    # Final sanity conversion.
    final_arr = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(final_arr), final_arr, 0.0)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s_final = float(final_arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s_final) or s_final < 0.01:
        final_arr = uniform_vec(1024)
        best_s = 2.0

    # Optional small validation only; do not spend time on huge direct convolutions.
    try:
        ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if ev is not None and final_arr.size <= 2048:
            val = ev([float(x) for x in final_arr])
            if np.isfinite(val):
                print(f"checked evaluator small final score {val:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"final fast score {best_s:.6f} length {final_arr.size}")
    return [float(x) for x in final_arr]
```
